You are auditing the enemy AI and encounter design of Voidsworn, a turn-based hex-fleet tactics roguelite set in deep space (browser, desktop and phones, solo developer). This pack contains the game's battle, content and map source with line numbers, the headless simulator (whose bot plays the player side, for contrast), and a simulation summary as an annex.

YOUR ONE TASK: judge the opponent. Cover: how the enemy AI chooses targets, moves, fires, uses abilities and Commands; whether it plays the objective (Hunt, Intercept, Hold, Breakthrough) or merely fights; whether the six factions' AIs actually behave differently (compare the doctrine data against the decision code that consumes it, and name any doctrine that is data-only, never enforced); how the AI handles squadrons, boarding, retreat and its own flagship's safety; and where a human player can exploit a fixed AI pattern (name the pattern and the counter it never learns). Then judge encounter design: the encounter set's variety, how map generation assembles fights, and whether difficulty comes from decisions or from stat scaling. Use the annex's win rates and loss figures as evidence where they bear on AI quality. Maximum 10 findings, most important first. For each finding about faction behaviour, state whether the difference lives in the decision code, the data tables, or only in the text.

RULES:
- Judge only what is in this pack. No web search, no outside knowledge of this game.
- Every finding must name the file, the line number, and quote the exact line (under 20 words). Sources below have line numbers prefixed to every line; cite those numbers.
- Mark every finding OBSERVED or INFERRED. Never claim a function, variable or line exists unless it appears in this pack.
- Be red team: adversarial, specific, no praise padding.
- End with a section titled "Not checked" listing what in the pack you did not examine, AND a section titled "Questions this pack cannot answer" listing every question your audit raised that the pack's contents cannot settle. Do not answer those questions from guesswork; list them.

122: function fleetPower(){ if(typeof RUN==='undefined'||!RUN||!RUN.fleet) return 99; let p=0; for(const sh of RUN.fleet){ if(sh.hp<=0) continue; const d=derive(sh), hp=Math.min(1,sh.hp/(d.maxHp||1)), cp=HULLS[sh.tpl].cp||1;

131: function genEnemyFleet(fac,s,col,kind,arch,mod){ arch=effArch(arch,fac);
132:   // each map column has a fixed difficulty, so a fleet that grows can outgrow it (the point of buying that cruiser)
133:   const F=FACTIONS[fac]; let budget=[0,4.2,9.5,14][s]+col*(s===1?0.9:s===2?1:1.1); if(kind==='elite') budget*=1.05;
134:   const list=[];
135:   if(kind==='boss'){ list.push({tpl:F.boss}); budget*=s===1?.28:.33; }
136:   // a soft floor only: a battered fleet meets up to 25% less, but a strong fleet never meets more
137:   if(typeof RUN!=='undefined'&&RUN&&RUN.fleet&&!(typeof NO_CAP!=='undefined'&&NO_CAP)){ const target=fleetPower()*(kind==='elite'?1:kind==='boss'?.5:.85)+.3;
138:     if(target<budget) budget=Math.max(target,budget*.75); }
139:   if(heatOn('h_hunted')) budget*=1.15;
140:   if(mod==='bounty') budget*=1.1;
141:   let roster=F.roster.map(id=>({id,cp:HULLS[id].cp,car:HULLS[id].ab==='launch'}));
142:   // archetypes reshape the same budget: a swarm of the lightest hulls, a heavy line, or a carrier with its escort
143:   if(arch==='swarm'){ const mn=Math.min(...roster.map(x=>x.cp)); const light=roster.filter(x=>x.cp<=mn+1&&!x.car); if(light.length) roster=light; }
144:   if(arch==='heavy'){ const big=roster.filter(x=>x.cp>=2&&(!x.car||!roster.some(y=>y.cp>=3&&!y.car))); if(big.length&&budget>=Math.min(...big.map(x=>x.cp))){ const top=big.filter(x=>x.cp<=budget+.3).sort((a,b)=>b.cp-a.cp)[0]; if(top){ list.push({tpl:top.id}); budget-=top.cp; } roster=big.concat(roster.filter(x=>x.cp<2&&!x.car).slice(0,1)); } }
145:   if(arch==='carrier'&&kind!=='boss'){ const cr=roster.filter(x=>x.car&&x.cp<=budget+.3).sort((a,b)=>b.cp-a.cp)[0]; if(cr){ list.push({tpl:cr.id}); budget-=cr.cp; roster=roster.filter(x=>!x.car); } }
146:   let g=0;
147:   while(budget>=1 && list.length<8 && g++<60){
148:     const opts=roster.filter(x=>x.cp<=budget+.3); if(!opts.length) break;
149:     const wts=opts.map(x=> arch==='heavy'?Math.pow(x.cp,1.6):x.cp===1?1.1:Math.min(2,budget/(x.cp*2))+.35);
150:     let r=rnd()*wts.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0), ch=opts[0];
151:     for(let i=0;i<opts.length;i++){ r-=wts[i]; if(r<=0){ch=opts[i];break;} }
152:     list.push({tpl:ch.id}); budget-=ch.cp;
153:   }
154:   return list;
155: }

236: function deploy(units, side, zoneFn){
237:   const mode=B.deployMode||'line', midR=Math.floor(BH/2), caps=units.filter(u=>!u.isSq).sort((a,b)=>b.size-a.size);
238:   let zone, facing=null;
239:   const A=ARENA, L0=A.mL, R0=BW-A.mR, T0=A.mT, T1=BH-A.mB; // the strip's own edges (the canvas is bigger)
240:   if(mode==='flank'){ if(side==='player'){ zone=h=>h.c>=L0+3&&h.c<=L0+9&&h.r>=T1-7; facing=5; } else { zone=h=>h.c>=R0-10&&h.c<=R0-4&&h.r<=T0+6; facing=2; } }
241:   else if(mode==='ambush'){ if(side==='player'){ zone=h=>h.c>=L0+5+CX&&h.c<=L0+9+CX&&Math.abs(h.r-midR)<=3; facing=0; } else zone=h=>h.c>=R0-14-CX&&h.c<=R0-6&&(h.r<=T0+2||h.r>=T1-3); }
242:   else { if(side==='player'){ zone=h=>h.c>=L0+4&&h.c<=L0+6&&Math.abs(h.r-midR)<=5; facing=0; } else { zone=h=>h.c>=R0-7&&h.c<=R0-5&&Math.abs(h.r-midR)<=5; facing=3; } }
243:   if(zoneFn) zone=zoneFn;
244:   const occ=occMap(), free=h=>!blocks(terr(h.q,h.r))&&!occ.has(hkey(h.q,h.r));
245:   let cand=boardHexes().map(h=>Object.assign(h,{c:h.q+Math.floor(h.r/2)})).filter(h=>zone(h)&&free(h));
246:   if(cand.length<caps.length+1){ const half=side==='player'?h=>h.c<BW/2-1:h=>h.c>=BW/2+1; cand=boardHexes().map(h=>Object.assign(h,{c:h.q+Math.floor(h.r/2)})).filter(h=>half(h)&&free(h)); } // a board shape left the usual zone too small
247:   const cq=cand.reduce((a,h)=>a+h.q,0)/cand.length, cr=cand.reduce((a,h)=>a+h.r,0)/cand.length, centre={q:Math.round(cq),r:Math.round(cr)};
248:   const target=offToAx(Math.floor(BW/2),midR), placed=[];

250:     for(const h of cand){ if(placed.some(p=>p.q===h.q&&p.r===h.r)) continue; const dmin=placed.length?Math.min(...placed.map(p=>hdist(p,h))):3; const sc=Math.min(dmin,2)*100-hdist(h,centre)*4+rnd(); if(sc>bs){bs=sc;best=h;} }
251:     if(!best) best=cand[0]; placed.push(best); u.q=best.q; u.r=best.r;

256: // v47: deployment zones placed by angle around the arena's centre, so the front can run any direction
257: function approachDeploy(){ const ap=B.approach, hs=boardHexes(); if(!hs.length) return; let cx=0,cz=0; for(const h of hs){ const p=hexXZ(h); cx+=p.x; cz+=p.z; } cx/=hs.length; cz/=hs.length;

267:   // everyone faces the middle of the fight, not just the board centre

270: // v47 gravity well (dead star maps): ships near it get a slingshot (+1 movement); end a turn close and it drags you in; on the rim, tidal stress tears at the hull
271: const WELL_RIM=3, WELL_PULL=5, WELL_ASSIST=6, WELL_DMG=7;
272: function wellTick(u,dq){ const W=B&&B.well; if(!W||u.isSq||u.dead) return; const d=hdist(u,W); u.gravAssist=false;
273:   if(d<=WELL_ASSIST){ u.mp+=1; u.gravAssist=true; }
274:   if(d<=WELL_RIM){ u.hp-=WELL_DMG; if(typeof fxText==='function') fxText(u,'Tidal stress \u2212'+WELL_DMG,'#c79bff'); if(u.hp<=0){ u.hp=0; log(`${u.name} is torn apart by the dead star's gravity.`, u.side==='player'?'e':'p'); u.killCause='tidal'; queueDeath(u,dq); return 'dead'; } }
275:   else if(d<=WELL_PULL){ const occ=occMap(); let best=null; for(const dd of DIRS){ const n={q:u.q+dd[0],r:u.r+dd[1]}; if(hdist(n,W)!==d-1||!onBoard(n.q,n.r)||blocks(terr(n.q,n.r))||occ.has(hkey(n.q,n.r))) continue; best=n; break; }
276:     if(best){ const from={q:u.q,r:u.r}; u.q=best.q; u.r=best.r; if(typeof fxGravPull==='function') fxGravPull(u,from); if(typeof fxText==='function') fxText(u,'Dragged by gravity','#c79bff'); } } }
277: function aiWellScore(u,v){ const W=B&&B.well; if(!W||u.isSq) return 0; const d=hdist(v,W); return d<=WELL_RIM?-45:d<=WELL_PULL?-6:0; }
278: function B0small(cfg){ if(typeof process!=='undefined'&&process.env&&process.env.NOCOMPACT) return false; return cfg.kind!=='boss'&&!!(RUN&&RUN.fleet)&&RUN.fleet.filter(x=>x.hp>0).length<=2; }
279: // no fleet starts boxed in: clear rocks and wrecks around each ship and along a lane toward the enemy

348: function gainResolve(side,n){ if(!B.resolve) B.resolve={player:0,enemy:0}; B.resolve[side]=Math.min(5,(B.resolve[side]||0)+n); }
349: function escortsBreak(){ if(!B.cfg||B.cfg.kind!=='boss'||B.escortsFled) return; const boss=B.units.find(u=>u.boss&&u.side==='enemy'&&!u.dead); if(!boss||boss.hp>boss.maxHp*.5) return;

353: function startSideTurn(side){
354:   const deaths=[]; // v53: ships killed by the turn's ticks; the caller awaits the returned promise so their deaths finish in order
355:   if(side==='player'&&B.round>1){ eventTick(); objectiveTick(deaths); }
356:   if(side==='enemy'){ try{ aiRefreshFocus(); }catch(e){} try{ bossCmdTick(); }catch(e){} } // v53-D: the fleet focus is kept (sticky) and re-checked, not wiped, each enemy turn
357:   escortsBreak();
358:   gainResolve(side,1); let burned=false;
359:   for(const u of B.units){
360:     if(u.dead||u.side!==side) continue;
361:     u.braced=false; u.pivotUsed=false; u.lockOn=false; u.focus=false; u.fullAhead=false; u.noTurn=false; u.evBuff=0; u.order=null; u.ramBoost=0;
362:     u.mp=Math.max(1,u.speed-u.st.engine); u.turnsLeft=u.turns;
363:     for(const w of u.weapons){ w.fired=false; if(w.cdLeft>0) w.cdLeft--; if(w.off>0) w.off--; }
364:     if(u.abCd>0) u.abCd--;
365:     if(u.st.shieldOff>0) u.st.shieldOff--; else if(u.shMax>0){ const rg=u.regen*(((typeof B!=='undefined'&&B&&B.condRegen)||1)); for(let i=0;i<4;i++) u.sh[i]=Math.min(u.shMax,u.sh[i]+rg); }
366:     if(u.hullRegen && u.hp<u.maxHp && !u.pyre) u.hp=Math.min(u.maxHp,u.hp+u.hullRegen);
367:     if(u.st.acid>0&&!u.isSq){ u.st.acid--; u.hp-=4; fxText(u,'Acid \u22124','#c8e04a',-12); if(u.hp<=0){ u.hp=0; burned=true; log(`${u.name} dissolves.`, u.side==='player'?'e':'p'); u.killCause='melt'; queueDeath(u,deaths); } }
368:     if(u.st.fire>0&&!u.dead){ const fd=u.st.fireDmg||6; u.st.fire--; u.hp-=fd; fxText(u,'Fire \u2212'+fd,'#f08a4b'); if(u.hp<=0){ u.hp=0; burned=true; log(`${u.name} burns out.`, u.side==='player'?'e':'p'); u.killCause='dark'; queueDeath(u,deaths); } if(!u.st.fire) u.st.fireDmg=0; }
369:     u.hexMoved=0; if(u.fx&&u.fx.stormRegen&&u.shMax>0&&!(u.st.shieldOff>0)){ for(let i=0;i<4;i++) u.sh[i]=Math.min(u.shMax,u.sh[i]+Math.round(u.shMax*u.fx.stormRegen)); }
370:     u.intercepts = u.weapons.some(w=>w.k==='flak')?2:0;
371:     u.boarded=false; u.rammed=false; u.emerg=false; u.repel=false;
372:     if(terr(u.q,u.r)==='storm'&&!u.isSq&&u.shMax>0){ for(let i=0;i<4;i++) u.sh[i]=Math.max(0,u.sh[i]-10); fxText(u,'Ion storm','#9fb8ff',-24); }
373:     if(u.crewMax&&u.crew<=0){ u.mp=0; u.turnsLeft=0; for(const w of u.weapons) w.fired=true; }
374:     if(B.well&&wellTick(u,deaths)==='dead') burned=true;
375:   }
376:   if(B.event&&B.event.k==='flare'&&B.round===B.event.r&&B.event.done) for(const u of B.units){ if(!u.dead&&u.side===side&&u.shMax>0) u.sh=[0,0,0,0]; }
377:   if(burned) checkEnd(); // a ship that burned out can decide the battle at the turn boundary
378:   return deaths.length?settleDeaths(deaths):null;
379: }
380: // v53: a death from a rules tick (fire, acid, the dead star, station guns) starts now, so its state changes land at once, and is awaited in order by the turn flow
381: function queueDeath(u,dq){ const p=Promise.resolve(killUnit(u,null)).catch(e=>console.error(e)); if(dq) dq.push(p); return p; }
382: async function settleDeaths(dq){ for(const p of dq) await p; }

384: // ---------- movement ----------
385: function passable(u,q,r,occ){ if(!onBoard(q,r)) return false; if(blocks(terr(q,r))) return false;
386:   const o=occ.get(hkey(q,r)); if(o && o.side!==u.side && !u.isSq) return false; return true; }
387: // v50 thrusters: besides burning forward, a hull can slip into either forward-diagonal hex or back off one hex while
388: // holding its heading, and can climb over or dive under a rock, wreck or enemy ship it cannot stop on.
389: // Each faction bends one of these rules (MOVE_RULES in data.js).
390: const MOVE_BASE={slip:2,rev:2,over:1,evadeAt:3,evade:5,still:10,rake:.1};
391: function moveRules(u){ return Object.assign({},MOVE_BASE,(u&&!u.isSq&&MOVE_RULES[u.fac])||{}); }
392: function canEnd(u,q,r,occ){ if(!onBoard(q,r)||blocks(terr(q,r))) return false; const o=occ.get(hkey(q,r)); return !o||o===u; }
393: function computeReach(u){
394:   const map=new Map(), states=[];
395:   const occ=occMap(), R=moveRules(u);
396:   const sk=s=>s.q+','+s.r+','+s.f+','+s.t;
397:   const start={q:u.q,r:u.r,f:u.facing,t:0,cost:0,path:[]};
398:   const seen=new Map([[sk(start),0]]); const queue=[start]; let qi=0;
399:   const pivotFree=R.pivot&&!u.pivotUsed;
400:   while(qi<queue.length){
401:     const s=queue[qi++]; if(seen.get(sk(s))<s.cost) continue; states.push(s);
402:     if(s.cost>=u.mp) continue;
403:     const nexts=[];
404:     if(u.isSq){ for(let f=0;f<6;f++){ const d=DIRS[f]; const nq=s.q+d[0], nr=s.r+d[1];
405:       if(passable(u,nq,nr,occ)) nexts.push({q:nq,r:nr,f,t:s.t,cost:s.cost+1,path:[...s.path,{q:nq,r:nr,f}]}); } }
406:     else {
407:       const opts=[[s.f,1,null],[(s.f+1)%6,R.slip,'slip'],[(s.f+5)%6,R.slip,'slip'],[(s.f+3)%6,R.rev,'rev']];
408:       for(const [df,c0,kind] of opts){ const d=DIRS[df], nq=s.q+d[0], nr=s.r+d[1]; if(!onBoard(nq,nr)) continue;
409:         // a heavy hull with too little movement can still spend all of it on one thruster hop
410:         let c=kind&&s.cost===0?Math.min(c0,u.mp):c0; let over=0; const tt=terr(nq,nr);
411:         if(blocks(tt)){ if(tt!=='rock'&&tt!=='hulk') continue; over=1; }
412:         const o=occ.get(hkey(nq,nr)); if(o&&o.side!==u.side) over=over||-1; // climb over terrain, dive under ships
413:         if(over) c+=R.over;
414:         if(s.cost+c>u.mp) continue;
415:         const st={q:nq,r:nr,f:s.f}; if(kind) st[kind]=1; if(kind==='slip') st.side=df===(s.f+1)%6?1:-1; if(over) st.over=over;
416:         nexts.push({q:nq,r:nr,f:s.f,t:s.t,cost:s.cost+c,path:[...s.path,st],slips:(s.slips||0)+(kind==='slip'?1:0),revs:(s.revs||0)+(kind==='rev'?1:0),overs:(s.overs||0)+(over?1:0)}); }
417:     }
418:     if(!u.isSq && !u.noTurn && s.t<u.turnsLeft){
419:       const tc=pivotFree&&s.t===0?0:1; if(s.cost+tc<=u.mp||tc===0)
420:       for(const dd of [1,-1]){ const f=(s.f+dd+6)%6; nexts.push({q:s.q,r:s.r,f,t:s.t+1,cost:s.cost+tc,path:[...s.path,{q:s.q,r:s.r,f,rot:true}],slips:s.slips,revs:s.revs,overs:s.overs}); }
421:     }
422:     for(const n of nexts){ const k=sk(n); if(seen.has(k)&&seen.get(k)<=n.cost) continue; seen.set(k,n.cost); queue.push(n); }
423:   }
424:   const ok=states.filter(s=>seen.get(sk(s))===s.cost&&canEnd(u,s.q,s.r,occ));
425:   for(const s of ok){ if(s.q===u.q&&s.r===u.r) continue; const k=hkey(s.q,s.r);
426:     const c=map.get(k); if(!c || s.cost<c.cost || (s.cost===c.cost&&s.t<c.t)) map.set(k,s); }
427:   return {map, states:ok};
428: }
429: // v51 fire on the move: opts.onLate runs about two-thirds of the way through the burn, after the rules have already put the ship
430: // on its destination hex, so guns open up while the hull is still travelling (the maths is identical to firing after the move)
431: async function moveUnit(u, state, opts){
432:   if(!inThisBattle(u)) return; const live=liveFrom(null);
433:   const path=state.path; let committed=false;
434:   const commit=()=>{ if(committed) return; committed=true; for(const st of path) if(!st.rot){ u.q=st.q; u.r=st.r; } u.facing=path.at(-1).f;
435:     u.mp=Math.max(0,u.mp-state.cost); u.turnsLeft-=state.t; if(state.t>0&&moveRules(u).pivot) u.pivotUsed=true; u.moved=true; u.hexMoved=(u.hexMoved||0)+path.filter(p=>!p.rot).length; if(B.usage&&u.side==='player'&&!u.isSq) B.usage.moves++; };
436:   let lateP=null; const onLate=opts&&opts.onLate?()=>{ if(!live()) return; commit(); try{ lateP=Promise.resolve(opts.onLate()).catch(e=>console.error(e)); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } }:null;
437:   if(typeof animPath==='function'){ await animPath(u,path,onLate?{onLate}:undefined); if(B) B.prog=(B.prog||0)+1; if(lateP) await lateP; if(!live()) return; if(committed){ objAfterMove(u); return; } } // one continuous burn
438:   else for(const st of path){
439:     if(st.rot){ await animRotate(u, st.f); }
440:     else { await animMove(u, st.q, st.r, st.f); }
441:     if(B) B.prog=(B.prog||0)+1; // progress for the busy watchdog
442:     if(!live()) return;
443:   }
444:   commit();
445:   objAfterMove(u);
446: }
447: function freePivot(u){ return !!(moveRules(u).pivot&&!u.pivotUsed); }
448: function canRotate(u){ return !u.dead && !u.isSq && !u.noTurn && u.turnsLeft>0 && (u.mp>0||freePivot(u)) && !u.braced; }
449: async function rotateUnit(u,dir){ if(!canRotate(u)||!inThisBattle(u)) return; const live=liveFrom(u), f=(u.facing+dir+6)%6; if(freePivot(u)) u.pivotUsed=true; else u.mp--; u.turnsLeft--; u.moved=true; await animRotate(u,f); if(!live()) return; u.facing=f; } // v53: facing commits after the live check, like moveUnit

451: // ---------- combat math ----------
452: function sightBlocked(q,r){ return blocks(terr(q,r))||!!(SOLID&&SOLID.has(q+','+r)); }
453: function los(a,b){ const line=hexLine(a,b); for(let i=1;i<line.length-1;i++){ if(sightBlocked(line[i].q,line[i].r)) return false; } return true; }
454: function weaponReady(w){ return !w.fired && w.cdLeft===0 && w.off===0; }
455: function inReach(u,w,t){ const d=hdist(u,t); if(d<1||d>w.r) return false; if(!inArc(u,t,w.arc)) return false; return los(u,t); }
456: function canFire(u,w,t){ if(u.dead||t.dead||u.side===t.side||u.braced||w.k==='flak') return false; if(!weaponReady(w)) return false; return inReach(u,w,t); }
457: // v50: a hull that sat still last turn is an easy solution; one that burned hard is not
458: function motionHit(t){ if(!t||t.isSq||t.hexMoved==null||t.braced) return 0; const R=moveRules(t); if(t.hexMoved===0) return R.still; if(t.hexMoved>=R.evadeAt) return -R.evade; return 0; }
459: function rakeBonus(u,w){ if(!u||u.isSq||!(w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S')||(u.hexMoved||0)<2) return 0; return moveRules(u).rake; }
460: function hitChance(u,w,t){
461:   const d=hdist(u,t), over=Math.max(0,d-w.opt-(u.fx.longAim||0));
462:   let c=w.acc+u.aim+(u.lockOn?15:0)-(u.fullAhead?15:0)-over*w.fall-Math.min(45,t.ev+(t.evBuff||0))*(w.evMult||1); // evasion caps at 45
463:   if(t.isSq && w.k!=='flak' && w.k!=='sq') c-=15;
464:   if(terr(t.q,t.r)==='neb') c-=15;
465:   if(terr(t.q,t.r)==='debris') c-=10;
466:   if(terr(u.q,u.r)==='storm') c-=10;
467:   if(u.crewMax){ if(u.crew<u.crewMax*.5) c-=10; if(u.crew<u.crewMax*.25) c-=10; }
468:   if(t.fx&&t.fx.burnEv&&(t.hexMoved||0)>=3) c-=t.fx.burnEv;
469:   c+=motionHit(t);
470:   const surge=cmdActive(t.side,'surge'); // v53: the enemy's Surge Command (was a wrapper patched on below)
471:   if(u.side==='player'&&!u.isSq&&!(u.sureUsed>0)&&runFx().firstSure) return surge?75:95; // the rangefinder relic: every ship's first attack of the battle
472:   const h=clamp(Math.round(c),5,95); return surge?Math.max(5,h-20):h;
473: }
474: // a boon firing in battle: a patron-coloured callout with the boon's name, counted for the after-battle recap
475: function boonFx(u,id,txt,dy){ try{ if(B){ B.boonStats=B.boonStats||{}; B.boonStats[id]=(B.boonStats[id]||0)+1; } const b=BOONS[id], P=b&&(b.p?PATRONS[b.p]:PATRONS[b.duo[0]]); fxText(u,(b?b.n+': ':'')+txt,P?P.col:'#f0b35a',dy==null?-30:dy); }catch(e){} }
476: function ownerFx(u){ if(!u) return {}; if(u.isSq){ const p=B&&B.units.find(x=>x.id===u.parent); return (p&&p.fx)||{}; } return u.fx||{}; }
477: // hull damage taken is cut when a ship holds the line next to an ally (Unbroken Line) or sits beside a Bulwark flagship
478: function guardMul(t){ if(!B||t.isSq) return 1; let m=1; const near=B.units.filter(x=>!x.dead&&x!==t&&x.side===t.side&&!x.isSq&&hdist(x,t)===1); if(!near.length) return 1;
479:   if(t.fx&&t.fx.lineGuard) m*=1-Math.min(.5,t.fx.lineGuard); const aura=Math.max(0,...near.map(x=>(x.fx&&x.fx.guardAura)||0)); if(aura) m*=1-aura; return m; }
480: function ionOf(att,w){ const f=ownerFx(att); return (w.ion||1)+(f.ionPlus||0)+(w.k==='lance'?(f.ionLance||0):0); }
481: function dmgMult(u,w,t,rear){
482:   let m=u.dmg;
483:   if(w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S') m+=u.fx.broadPct||0;
484:   if(w.k==='lance') m+=u.fx.lancePct||0;
485:   if(w.k==='torp') m+=u.fx.torpPct||0;
486:   if(u.isSq) m+=u.sqPct||0;
487:   if(rear) m+=u.fx.rearPct||0;
488:   if(t.hp<t.maxHp/2) m+=u.fx.huntPct||0;
489:   if(u.focus) m+=.3;
490:   if(u.fx.stillPct&&!u.hexMoved&&(w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S'||w.k==='battery')) m+=u.fx.stillPct;
491:   if(u.fx.momPct) m+=Math.min(4,u.hexMoved||0)*u.fx.momPct;
492:   if(u.fx.prowPct&&w.arc==='F') m+=u.fx.prowPct;
493:   m+=rakeBonus(u,w);
494:   let base=w.dmg*m;
495:   base*= t.isSq ? (w.vsSq||1) : (w.vsCap||1);
496:   // v53: bloodied, Commands and volley multipliers (were a wrapper patched on below; same order, same numbers)
497:   if(u.bloodied&&!u.isSq) base*=1+.15*u.bloodied; if(cmdActive(u.side,'hunt')) base*=1+cmdPow('hunt',null,u.side); if(u.isSq&&cmdActive(u.side,'scramble')) base*=1+cmdPow('scramble',null,u.side); if(B&&B.volleyMul&&B.volleyMul.side===u.side) base*=B.volleyMul.m;
498:   return base;
499: }
500: // Expected value used for previews and AI
501: function expectedVs(u,w,t){
502:   const quad=shieldQuad(t,u), rear=quad===2;
503:   const hit=hitChance(u,w,t)/100, per=dmgMult(u,w,t,rear);
504:   const raw=per*w.shots*hit;
505:   const pierce=w.pierce||0, ion=ionOf(u,w);
506:   const shieldable=raw*(1-pierce);
507:   const absorbed=Math.min(shieldable,(t.sh[quad]||0)/ion);
508:   let hull=(raw-absorbed)*(1-(rear?t.armor*.5:t.armor));
509:   if(t.braced) hull*=Math.max(.15,1-.4-(t.fx.bracePlus||0));
510:   hull*=cmdTaken(t)*guardMul(t);
511:   return {hit, raw, hull, shield:absorbed*ion, quad, rear};
512: }
513: function applyHit(att,w,t,dmg,quad){
514:   const rear=quad===2, pierce=w.pierce||0, ion=ionOf(att,w), of=ownerFx(att);
515:   let shieldable=dmg*(1-pierce), through=dmg-shieldable;
516:   const s=t.sh[quad]||0; const absorbed=Math.min(shieldable,s/ion);
517:   t.sh[quad]=Math.max(0,s-absorbed*ion); shieldable-=absorbed;
518:   const arm=Math.max(0,t.armor-(t.shredded||0))*(1-(w.armorPierce||0)); let hull=(through+shieldable)*(1-(rear?arm*.5:arm)); // v48: spines strip armor, gauss ignores half of it
519:   if(t.braced) hull*=Math.max(.15,1-.4-(t.fx.bracePlus||0));
520:   hull*=cmdTaken(t)*guardMul(t);
521:   let crit=false;
522:   if(hull>0.5 && rnd()*100 < (w.crit||0)+att.crit+(att.lockOn?10:0)+(rear?15:0)+((att.hexMoved||0)>=3?(of.momCrit||0):0)){ hull*=1.5+(of.critMul||0); crit=true; }
523:   hull=Math.round(hull);
524:   if(hull>0&&!t.isSq&&!t.dead){ const ch=(of.igniteCh||0)+((w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S')?(of.broadIgnite||0):0)+(att.isSq?(of.sqIgnite||0):0);
525:     if(ch>0&&rnd()<ch){ const fd=6+(of.fireDmg||0); if(!(t.st.fire>0)) boonFx(t,att.isSq&&of.sqIgnite?'d_burnswarm':(w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S')&&of.broadIgnite&&!of.igniteCh?'d_inferno':'e_kindle','ignited',-28); t.st.fire=Math.max(t.st.fire||0,2+(of.fireLong||0)); t.st.fireDmg=Math.max(t.st.fireDmg||6,fd); } }
526:   if(hull>0&&!t.isSq&&!t.dead){ if(w.ignite){ t.st.fire=Math.max(t.st.fire||0,2); t.st.fireDmg=Math.max(t.st.fireDmg||6,6); } // v48 traits
527:     if(w.shred) t.shredded=Math.min(.15,(t.shredded||0)+w.shred);
528:     if(w.acid){ t.st.acid=2; } }
529:   if(of.pyre&&!t.isSq&&t.hp<t.maxHp/2) t.pyre=true;
530:   t.hp-=hull; att.dealt+=hull;
531:   damageSection(t, t._calledSec||SEC_OF_QUAD[quad], hull, att);
532:   return {shield:Math.round(absorbed*ion), hull, crit};
533: }
534: const SEC_OF_QUAD=['prow','mid','eng','mid'], SEC_NAME={prow:'prow',mid:'midships',eng:'engines'};
535: // v41: Restore mends destroyed sections too, giving back what the break took
536: function repairSection(t,sec,frac){ const s=t.sec&&t.sec[sec]; if(!s) return false;
537:   if(!s.broken){ if(s.hp>0) s.hp=Math.min(s.max,s.hp+Math.round(s.max*frac)); return false; }
538:   s.broken=false; s.hp=Math.max(1,Math.round(s.max*frac));
539:   if(sec==='prow') t.weapons.forEach(w=>{ if(w.arc==='F'&&w.off>=99) w.off=0; });
540:   if(sec==='mid'){ t.weapons.forEach(w=>{ if((w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S')&&w.off>=99) w.off=0; }); if(t.shMax>0&&t.st.shieldOff>=99) t.st.shieldOff=0; }
541:   if(sec==='eng'){ t.st.engine=0; if(t.secSave){ t.turns=t.secSave.turns; t.ev=t.secSave.ev; t.secSave=null; } }
542:   return true; }
543: function damageSection(t,sec,dmg,by){
544:   if(!t.sec||!t.sec[sec]||dmg<=0) return; const s=t.sec[sec];
545:   if(t.crewMax) t.crew=Math.max(0,t.crew-Math.round(dmg/t.maxHp*t.crewMax*.35));
546:   if(s.hp<=0) return; s.hp=Math.max(0,s.hp-dmg); if(s.hp>0||s.broken) return; s.broken=true; if(typeof fxSectionDown==='function') fxSectionDown(t,sec);
547:   if(sec==='prow') t.weapons.forEach(w=>{ if(w.arc==='F') w.off=999; });
548:   if(sec==='mid'){ t.weapons.forEach(w=>{ if(w.arc==='P'||w.arc==='S') w.off=999; }); if(t.shMax>0){ t.st.shieldOff=999; t.sh=[0,0,0,0]; } }
549:   if(sec==='eng'){ t.secSave={turns:t.turns,ev:t.ev}; t.st.engine=Math.max(0,t.speed-1); t.turns=Math.min(t.turns,1); t.ev=0; }
550:   fxText(t,SEC_NAME[sec][0].toUpperCase()+SEC_NAME[sec].slice(1)+' destroyed','#f0b35a',-36);
551:   log(`${t.name}: ${SEC_NAME[sec]} destroyed. ${sec==='prow'?'Forward weapons silenced.':sec==='mid'?'Broadsides and shield generator gone.':'Engines crippled.'}`, t.side==='player'?'e':'p');
552:   if(by&&!by.dead) gainResolve(by.side,1);
553: }
554: function systemDamage(t,forced){
555:   if(t.isSq||t.dead) return null;
556:   const map={engines:'engine',shields:'shields',weapons:'weapon',eng:'engine',mid:'shields',prow:'weapon'};
557:   let r=forced&&map[forced]?map[forced]:pick(['engine','shields','weapon','fire']);
558:   if(r==='shields'&&!(t.shMax>0)) r='fire';
559:   if(r==='weapon'&&!t.weapons.length) r='fire';
560:   if(r==='weapon'&&forced){ const live=t.weapons.filter(w=>w.off===0).sort((a,b)=>b.dmg*b.shots-a.dmg*a.shots); if(live.length){ live[0].off=2; return live[0].n+' knocked out'; } }
561:   if(r==='engine'){ t.st.engine=Math.min(t.speed-1,t.st.engine+1); return 'Engines hit'; }
562:   if(r==='shields'&&t.shMax>0){ t.st.shieldOff=2; return 'Shield generator down'; }
563:   if(r==='weapon'){ const w=pick(t.weapons); w.off=2; return w.n+' knocked out'; }
564:   t.st.fire=2; return 'Fire on deck';
565: }

567: async function fireWeapon(u,wi,t,opts={}){ if(!inThisBattle(u)) return false; const live=liveFrom(null);
568:   const w=u.weapons[wi];
569:   if(!opts.intercept && !canFire(u,w,t)) return false;
570:   const keep=[w.fired,w.cdLeft,u.sureUsed], b0=B; // v53: an abandoned shot gives the gun back (see the live check below)
571:   if(!opts.intercept){ w.fired=true; if(w.cd) w.cdLeft=w.cd; if(u.side==='player'&&!u.isSq) u.sureUsed=(u.sureUsed||0)+1; }
572:   if(B.usage&&u.side==='player'&&!opts.intercept&&!u.isSq){ const k=(ARC_NAME&&ARC_NAME[w.arc])||String(w.arc); B.usage.fire[k]=(B.usage.fire[k]||0)+1; if(opts.called) B.usage.aim++; }
573:   // squadrons attacking capital ships run the flak gauntlet first
574:   if(u.isSq && !t.isSq && !opts.intercept){
575:     for(const d of alive(t.side)){
576:       if(d.isSq||d.intercepts<=0||d.braced) continue;
577:       const fi=d.weapons.findIndex(x=>x.k==='flak'&&x.off===0); if(fi<0) continue;
578:       if(hdist(d,u)>d.weapons[fi].r) continue;
579:       d.intercepts--;
580:       await fireWeapon(d,fi,u,{intercept:true});
581:       if(u.dead){ return true; }
582:     }
583:   }
584:   const quad=shieldQuad(t,u), shBefore=(t.sh&&t.sh[quad])||0;
585:   const res={shield:0,hull:0,crits:0,hits:0,misses:0,sys:null};
586:   const called=opts.called&&!t.isSq&&!opts.intercept?opts.called:null;
587:   const hc=hitChance(u,w,t);
588:   const shots=[];
589:   for(let i=0;i<w.shots;i++) shots.push(rnd()*100<hc);
590:   if(w.rockets&&!t.isSq){ let shot=0; for(const d of alive(t.side)){ if(d.isSq||d.intercepts<=0||d.braced) continue; const fw=d.weapons.find(x=>x.k==='flak'&&!(x.off>0)); if(!fw||hdist(d,t)>fw.r) continue; // point defense near the target picks off rockets
591:       for(let i=0;i<shots.length&&d.intercepts>0;i++) if(shots[i]&&rnd()<.55){ shots[i]=false; d.intercepts--; shot++; } }
592:     if(shot){ res.pd=shot; fxText(t,shot+' rocket'+(shot>1?'s':'')+' shot down','#bfe3ff',30); } }
593:   await animWeapon(u,w,t,shots);
594:   if(B) B.prog=(B.prog||0)+1;
595:   if(!live()){ if(!opts.intercept&&B===b0){ w.fired=keep[0]; w.cdLeft=keep[1]; u.sureUsed=keep[2]; } return false; } // abandoned after a stall: the shot never lands, and the gun is not spent
596:   if(called){ u.crit+=15; t._calledSec=called; } // v45: applied only once the shot is known to land, so a stall can never leave it behind
597:   for(const h of shots){
598:     if(t.dead) break;
599:     if(!h){ res.misses++; continue; }
600:     res.hits++;
601:     const d=dmgMult(u,w,t,quad===2)*(0.85+rnd()*.3);
602:     const r=applyHit(u,w,t,d,quad);
603:     res.shield+=r.shield; res.hull+=r.hull; if(r.crit){ res.crits++; const cs=ownerFx(u).critSys; if(!res.sys && (called||cs||rnd()<.4)){ res.sys=systemDamage(t,called); if(cs&&res.sys) boonFx(t,'a_crit',res.sys,-48); } }
604:   }
605:   if(called){ u.crit-=15; t._calledSec=null; }
606:   if(res.shield>0){ fxShield(t,quad); fxText(t,'\u2212'+res.shield+' shield','#62ccff',-18); if(t.shMax>0&&t.sh[quad]<=.5){ fxShieldBreak(t,quad); fxText(t,'Shields down','#9fe2ff',-40);
607:       const ar=ownerFx(u).arcShort; if(ar&&shBefore>.5&&!t.dead){ t.hp-=ar; res.hull+=ar; u.dealt+=ar; boonFx(t,'s_arc','\u2212'+ar,-56); } } }
608:   if(res.hull>0) fxText(t,'\u2212'+res.hull+' hull',res.crits?'#ffd27a':'#ffae42',0);
609:   if(res.hits===0) fxText(t,'Miss','#9a9384',0);
610:   if(res.crits){ fxText(t,'Critical','#f0b35a',18); if(typeof fxCrit==='function') fxCrit(t); }
611:   const who = u.side==='player'?'p':'e';
612:   const parts=[];
613:   if(res.pd) parts.push(`${res.pd} shot down by point defense`);
614:   if(res.hits===0) parts.push('all shots miss');
615:   else { if(res.shield) parts.push(`${res.shield} to ${QUAD_NAME[quad]} shields`); if(res.hull) parts.push(`${res.hull} hull`); if(!res.shield&&!res.hull) parts.push('no damage'); }
616:   if(res.crits) parts.push(res.crits>1?`${res.crits} criticals`:'critical');
617:   log(`${u.name} ${opts.intercept?'intercepts with':'fires'} ${w.n} at ${t.name}: ${parts.join(', ')}.${res.sys?' '+res.sys+'!':''}`, who);
618:   if(res.hits>0&&t.braced&&!t.dead&&!u.dead&&!opts.intercept&&t.side!==u.side){ const rp=ownerFx(t).riposte; if(rp){ u.hp-=rp; t.dealt=(t.dealt||0)+rp; boonFx(u,'i_brace','\u2212'+rp,-40); if(u.hp<=0) await killUnit(u,t); } } // Iron Discipline: braced ships strike back
619:   if(t.hp<=0){ t.killCause=killCauseOf(w,t); await killUnit(t,u); }
620:   return true;
621: }
622: // a ram: any enemy ship dead ahead of the bow, with at least one clear hex of run-up and movement left to cover the gap
623: function ramInfo(u){
624:   if(!u||u.isSq||u.braced||u.dead||u.rammed||(u.crewMax&&u.crew<=0)) return null; const left=Math.min(u.mp,Math.max(1,u.speed-u.st.engine)); if(left<1) return null;
625:   const d=DIRS[u.facing]; let q=u.q,r=u.r; const path=[];
626:   for(let i=0;i<=left;i++){ const nq=q+d[0], nr=r+d[1]; if(!onBoard(nq,nr)||blocks(terr(nq,nr))) return null; const o=unitAt(nq,nr);
627:     if(o&&o.id!==u.id){ return (o.side!==u.side&&!o.isSq&&path.length>=1)?{t:o,path}:null; } path.push({q:nq,r:nr,f:u.facing}); q=nq; r=nr; }
628:   return null;
629: }
630: // could this ship ram from a reachable hex and facing (used to mark ram spots on the move overlay)
631: function ramFrom(u,st){ if(!st||st.stay) return null; const v=Object.assign({},u,{q:st.q,r:st.r,facing:st.f,mp:u.mp-st.cost}); return ramInfo(v); }
632: // rams hit hard but can't one-shot a healthy ship: at most 40% of the target's full hull (finishing a damaged ship is still possible),
633: // the target's facing shield soaks part of it, and the rammer takes more back
634: function ramEstimate(u,t,run){ const f=u.fx||{}, base=(14+46*u.size)*(RAM_FAC[u.fac]?1.2:1)*(run==null?1:run<=1?.75:run===2?1:1.15), quad=shieldQuad(t,u);
635:   const raw=base*(1+(f.ramPct||0))*(u.ramBoost||1)*(quad===0?1:1.2)*(1-t.armor*.5), soak=Math.min(raw*.5,(t.sh&&t.sh[quad])||0);
636:   return {dmg:Math.round(Math.min(raw-soak,t.maxHp*(.4+Math.min(.3,f.ramCap||0)))), soak:Math.round(soak), recoil:Math.round(base*(RAM_FAC[u.fac]?.35:.5)*clamp(t.size/u.size,.5,1.6)*(1-u.armor*.5)*(1-Math.min(1,f.ramGuard||0))), sec:SEC_OF_QUAD[quad]}; }
637: async function ramUnit(u){
638:   const info=ramInfo(u); if(!info) return false; const t=info.t, live=liveFrom(u);
639:   if(typeof animPath==='function') await animPath(u,info.path,{charge:true}); else for(const p of info.path){ await animMove(u,p.q,p.r,p.f); }
640:   if(!live()) return false; // abandoned after a stall: the ram never lands
641:   for(const p of info.path){ u.q=p.q; u.r=p.r; } u.facing=info.path.at(-1).f; // v53: the ram commits its own run-up (animPath is presentation only)
642:   u.mp=0; u.turnsLeft=0; u.rammed=true; if(B.usage&&u.side==='player'){ B.usage.ram=(B.usage.ram||0)+1; for(const id of ['d_jugg','r_prow']) if(hasBoon(id)){ B.boonStats=B.boonStats||{}; B.boonStats[id]=(B.boonStats[id]||0)+1; } } const e=ramEstimate(u,t,info.path.length);

652: function boardTargets(u){ if(!u||u.isSq||u.braced||u.boarded||u.dead||!u.crewMax||u.crew<Math.max(4,u.crewMax*.4)||u.weapons.some(w=>w.fired)) return [];

654: // chance that a boarding action captures the target, from the same dice as boardUnit
655: // one capture model everywhere: boardUnit rolls r in [0.75,1.25); capture when round(crew*mult*r*.38) >= defenders.
656: // The shown chance is that roll's exact probability, so the number on the button is the real odds.
657: function captureChance(u,t){ const s=u.crew*(BOARD_MULT[u.fac]||1)*(1+((u.fx&&u.fx.boardPct)||0))*.38; if(s<=0) return 0; const x=(t.crew-.5)/s; return Math.round(100*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(1.25-x)/.5))); }
658: // why a selected ship can't shoot a target, in plain words
659: function cantFireReason(s,t){ if(s.braced) return 'This ship is braced, so it holds fire this turn.';
660:   const ready=s.weapons.filter(w=>weaponReady(w)); if(!ready.length) return 'Every weapon on this ship has already fired this turn.';
661:   const d=hdist(s,t), maxR=Math.max(...ready.map(w=>w.r)); const inR=ready.filter(w=>d<=w.r);
662:   if(!inR.length) return `Out of range: ${d} hexes away, your longest ready gun reaches ${maxR}.`;
663:   const inA=inR.filter(w=>inArc(s,t,w.arc)); if(!inA.length){ const arcs=[...new Set(inR.map(w=>(ARC_NAME&&ARC_NAME[w.arc])||w.arc))].join(' or '); return `In range, but no ready gun points at it. Turn so it sits in your ${arcs} arc.`; }
664:   if(!los(s,t)) return 'Line of sight is blocked by asteroids or wreckage.';
665:   return 'None of the ready guns can reach that target right now.'; }
666: function boardOdds(u,t){ const a=u.crew*(BOARD_MULT[u.fac]||1)*(1+((u.fx&&u.fx.boardPct)||0)), d=t.crew*(BOARD_MULT[t.fac]||1)*(t.repel?2.25:1.5); const p=captureChance(u,t); return {kill:Math.round(a*.38), loss:Math.round(d*.3), chance:p, capture:p>=50}; }
667: async function boardUnit(u,t){
668:   if(!boardTargets(u).includes(t)) return false;
669:   if(B.usage&&u.side==='player') B.usage.board=(B.usage.board||0)+1; u.boarded=true; u.repel=true; u.mp=0; u.weapons.forEach(w=>w.fired=true);
670:   const live=liveFrom(u); await fxBoard(u,t); if(!live()||t.dead) return false; // abandoned after a stall: the boarders never land
671:   const a=u.crew*(BOARD_MULT[u.fac]||1)*(1+((u.fx&&u.fx.boardPct)||0))*(.75+rnd()*.5), d=t.crew*(BOARD_MULT[t.fac]||1)*(t.repel?2.25:1.5)*(.75+rnd()*.5);
672:   const kill=Math.round(a*.38), loss=Math.min(u.crew-1,Math.round(d*.3)); t.crew=Math.max(0,t.crew-kill); u.crew=Math.max(1,u.crew-loss);
673:   const who=u.side==='player'?'p':'e';
674:   if(t.crew<=0&&t.side==='player'&&!t.captured){ t.crew=Math.max(2,Math.round(t.crewMax*.15)); const s1=systemDamage(t), s2=systemDamage(t); fxText(t,'Holds the bridge','#f0b35a',-30);
675:     log(`${u.name}'s boarders overrun ${t.name}, but the last of its crew hold the bridge.${s1?' 676:   if(t.crew<=0){ if(B.usage&&u.side==='player') B.usage.cap=(B.usage.cap||0)+1; const old=t.side; t.side=u.side; t.crew=Math.max(3,Math.round(t.crewMax*.4)); t.captured=true; t.activated=true; t.mp=0; t.turnsLeft=0; t.braced=false; t.weapons.forEach(w=>w.fired=true);
677:     gainResolve(u.side,2); fxText(t,'Captured','#f0b35a',-30); log(`Boarding parties from ${u.name} take ${t.name}. It now fights for ${u.side==='player'?'you':'the enemy'}.`, who);
678:     if(typeof onCapture==='function') onCapture(t,old); checkEnd(); return true; }

681:   log(`${u.name} boards ${t.name}: ${kill} defenders killed, ${loss} boarders lost.${extra}`, who); return true;

683: async function tacticRam(u){ const info=ramInfo(u); if(!info||info.path.length<2) return false; /* the AI only rams with a real run-up */ const e=ramEstimate(u,info.t,info.path.length), bold=!!RAM_FAC[u.fac];

685: async function tacticBoard(u){ let best=null,bv=0; for(const t of boardTargets(u)){ const o=boardOdds(u,t); const v=o.chance>=70?100+t.maxHp/10:0; if(v>bv){bv=v;best=t;} }

687: // v52: how a ship dies depends on what killed it (presentation only; fx3d animExplode reads u.killCause)
688: // v53: the one list of kill causes. killCauseOf, the turn ticks (melt, dark, tidal) and rams (big) set u.killCause to one of these ids;
689: // fx3d animExplode keys its death presets by the same ids (an id it lacks plays the plain blast), and salvage reads the words and whether the wreck is scrap
690: const KILL_CAUSES={cut:{w:'cut in two by a lance'},big:{w:'blown apart',scrap:1},flay:{w:'flayed by gauss fire',scrap:1},melt:{w:'dissolved',scrap:1},dark:{w:'gutted and dark'},blast:{w:'destroyed'},tidal:{w:'torn apart by the dead star',scrap:1}};
691: function killCauseOf(w,t){ if(!w) return 'blast'; const k=w.k, tr=w.trait;
692:   if(tr==='gauss'||k==='gauss'||k==='doom') return 'flay';

753: // v53-D: objective urgency (veteran): rises as the round clock runs down and when the station or the relay score is close to decided
754: function aiObjUrgency(o){ const done=clamp(((B.round||1)-1)/(o.rounds||ENC_ROUNDS),0,1);
755:   if(o.type==='defend') return 1+done*1.2+(o.hp<=2?.6:0);

758: function objectiveTick(dq){ const o=B.obj; if(!o||B.over) return;
759:   if(o.type==='defend'){ const near=caps('enemy').filter(u=>hdist(u,o.hex)<=2); if(near.length){ o.hp=Math.max(0,o.hp-near.length); log(`${near.length} enemy ship${near.length>1?'s':''} at the station. Integrity ${o.hp}/${o.hpMax}.`,'e'); }

765: // one owner for "the battle ended": the first time a battle becomes over, its end screen is scheduled once,
766: // whichever rule (a kill, the relay score, a squadron strike at round start, a Command) caused it
767: function checkEnd(){ const was=!!(B&&B.over); checkEndRules(); if(B&&!was&&B.over) scheduleBattleEnd(); }
768: function scheduleBattleEnd(){ const b=B; if(!b||b.showcase) return; Promise.resolve().then(()=>{ if(B===b&&b.over&&!b.ending&&typeof endBattleFlow==='function'){ b.busy=false; endBattleFlow(); } }); }
769: function checkEndRules(){
770:   if(B.over) return;
771:   if(caps('enemy').length===0){ B.over='win'; return; }
772:   const o=B.obj;
773:   if(o&&o.type==='break'){ const out=B.units.filter(u=>u.exited).length; o.out=out; if(out>=o.need){ B.over='win'; log(`${out} ship${out>1?'s':''} broke through. The fleet jumps clear.`,'s'); return; }
774:     if(out>0&&caps('player').length+out<o.need){ B.over='loss'; B.withdraw=true; log('Too few ships can still get through. The survivors jump clear.','e'); return; } }
775:   if(caps('player').length===0){ B.over='loss'; return; }
776:   if(o&&o.type==='intercept'){ const tr=B.units.filter(u=>u.transport); o.kill=tr.filter(u=>u.dead&&!u.fled).length; o.esc=tr.filter(u=>u.fled).length;
777:     if(o.kill>=o.need){ B.over='win'; B.routed=true; log('The convoy is broken. The escorts break off.','s'); return; }
778:     if(o.esc>o.total-o.need){ B.over='win'; B.partial=true; log('Enough transports got through. The escorts break off.','e'); return; } }

811: // If an action truly hangs, the battle's epoch goes up so that action can never act later.
812: function gameVisible(){ return typeof document==='undefined'||document.visibilityState!=='hidden'; }
813: function stallGuard(p,ms,tag,info){ let iv, done=false; const b0=B, gT=()=>(typeof G!=='undefined'&&G&&typeof G.time==='number')?G.time:null, g0=gT(); const lim=new Promise(res=>{ let used=0, last=Date.now(); iv=setInterval(()=>{ const now=Date.now(); if(gameVisible()) used+=now-last; last=now; if(done){ clearInterval(iv); return; } if(B!==b0){ clearInterval(iv); res('stale'); return; }
814:       // animations run on game time, so a slow renderer stretches them: count game time, with a 4x real-time backstop in case frames stop entirely
815:       const g=gT(); if(g!=null&&g0!=null?((g-g0)*1000<ms&&used<ms*4):used<ms) return; clearInterval(iv); // a guard only ever acts on its own battle
816:       try{ if(B) B.epoch=(B.epoch||0)+1; if(typeof tel==='function'){ tel('stall',Object.assign({tag,round:B&&B.round,side:B&&B.side,busy:B&&B.busy},info||{})); if(typeof Persist!=='undefined') Persist.saveLocal(); } /* v53: local only, no cloud stamp */

914: function canAbility(u){
915:   if(!u.ab||u.dead||u.abCd>0||u.braced) return false;
916:   if(u.ab==='surge'&&u.st&&u.st.shieldOff>=99) return false; // the shield generator section is destroyed
917:   if(u.ab==='launch') return squadronsOf(u).length<u.hangar && (u.spare==null||u.spare>0) && launchHexes(u).length>0;
918:   if(u.ab==='reload') return u.weapons.some(w=>w.k==='torp'&&(w.cdLeft>0||w.fired));
919:   if(u.ab==='regrow') return u.hp<u.maxHp;
920:   if(u.ab==='reclaim') return alive(u.side).some(x=>!x.isSq&&hdist(x,u)<=3&&x.hp<x.maxHp);
921:   if(u.ab==='blink') return blinkHexes(u).length>0;
922:   if(u.ab==='ionpulse') return alive(u.side==='player'?'enemy':'player').some(t=>!t.isSq&&hdist(t,u)<=4);
923:   return true;
924: }
925: // Ithar phase jump: any open, playable hex within 3, straight past whatever is in the way

927: // without a tapped hex (enemy ships, the sim bot): jump to the open hex that best lines up a shot, or away when badly hurt
928: function autoBlink(u){ const hs=blinkHexes(u), foes=alive(u.side==='player'?'enemy':'player').filter(x=>!x.isSq); if(!hs.length||!foes.length) return null; const hurt=u.hp<u.maxHp*.35; let best=null,bv=-1e9;
929:   for(const h of hs){ let v=-1e9; for(let f=0;f<6;f++){ const st=stateView(u,{q:h.q,r:h.r,f}); const o=evalOffense(st,u.weapons.filter(weaponReady),foes), th=evalThreat(st,foes); v=Math.max(v,hurt?-th:o-th*.3); } const near=Math.min(...foes.map(t=>hdist(h,t))); const val=v+(hurt?near*2:-near*.5); if(val>bv){bv=val;best=h;} }

975: function stateView(u,s){ const v=Object.create(u); v.q=s.q; v.r=s.r; v.facing=s.f; if(s.path&&!u.isSq) v.hexMoved=(u.hexMoved||0)+s.path.filter(p=>!p.rot).length; return v; }
976: function evalOffense(a, ready, targets){
977:   let tot=0;
978:   for(const w of ready){ let best=0;
979:     for(const t of targets){ if(!inReach(a,w,t)) continue; const e=expectedVs(a,w,t); let v=e.hull+e.shield*.3; if(e.hull>=t.hp) v+=15+t.maxHp*.1; if(t.isSq&&!w.vsSq) v*=.5; if(v>best) best=v; }
980:     tot+=best; }

983: // how each faction fights: threat weight (caution), weight when badly hurt, and a preferred range
984: // range>0 keeps at least that many hexes away (kiting); range<0 wants to be within that many hexes (closing in)

986:   voidsworn:{tw:1,hurt:1,range:0,rw:0},

994: // threat now includes what enemies could reach after moving, not just where they sit
995: // v53-D: guns that already fired this round (alternating) are spent; the after-move term needs the gun to bear after a one-step turn and a clear line

999: // can this gun still fire before the round ends? In alternating play a gun that fired (or a braced hull) is done until the next round; flak never fires at ships
1000: function aiGunLive(p,w){ if(w.k==='flak') return false; if(B&&B.alt&&(w.fired||p.braced)) return false; return true; }
1001: // would the target sit in this arc now, or after a one-step turn (if the ship has a turn)?

1003: // a position that lines up a ram for next activation

1005: // the fleet-wide focus target: the most valuable ship that is closest to dying
1006: function aiFocusTarget(){ const sh=aiShooters(); let best=null,bv=0; for(const t of alive('player')){ if(t.isSq) continue; const v=aiFocusVal(t,sh); if(v>bv){bv=v;best=t;} } return best; }
1007: // v53-D: value over effective hull, priced the way expectedVs will price it (brace, guard aura, Iron Command), and worth less when few of our guns can reach it this turn
1008: function aiShooters(){ return caps('enemy').filter(u=>!u.transport&&u.weapons.length); }
1009: function aiFocusVal(t,shooters){ if(!t||t.dead||t.isSq) return 0; const br=t.braced?Math.max(.15,1-.4-((t.fx&&t.fx.bracePlus)||0)):1, take=Math.max(.15,br*guardMul(t)*cmdTaken(t));

1011:   for(const u of shooters){ let rr=0; for(const w of u.weapons) if(w.cdLeft<=1&&w.off<=1&&w.k!=='flak'&&w.r>rr) rr=w.r; if(rr&&hdist(u,t)<=rr+(u.speed||2)) n++; }

1013: // sticky fleet focus (veteran only): kept until the target dies or another is clearly (25%) better; re-checked each enemy turn and activation, never overwritten by a single ship's volley
1014: function aiRefreshFocus(){ if(!B) return null; if(!aiVet()){ B.aiFocus=null; return null; } const sh=aiShooters(), cur=B.aiFocus&&!B.aiFocus.dead&&B.aiFocus.side==='player'&&inThisBattle(B.aiFocus)?B.aiFocus:null, cv=cur?aiFocusVal(cur,sh):0;

1016:   B.aiFocus=!cur||(best&&bv>cv*1.25)?(best||cur):cur; return B.aiFocus; }
1017: function evalOffenseVet(a,ready,targets,focus){ let tot=0;

1020: // alternating activations: veterans act with the ship that has the best opportunity, or the most danger, right now
1021: function aiPickActivation(list){ if(!aiVet()||list.length<2) return list[0]; aiRefreshFocus(); const foes=alive('player'); let best=list[0],bv=-1e9;

1027: function evalThreat(v, foes){
1028:   let tot=0;
1029:   for(const p of foes){ if(hdist(p,v)>9) continue;
1030:     for(const w of p.weapons){ if(w.cdLeft>1||w.off>1||!aiGunLive(p,w)) continue; if(hdist(p,v)>w.r) continue; if(!inArc(p,v,w.arc)) continue; const e=expectedVs(p,w,v); tot+=e.hull+e.shield*.2; } }

1033: // v53-D: the volley primary is re-picked before every gun from current hull and shields, with damage counted only up to the hull a target has left
1034: // (a 2-hull wreck is not worth a full broadside); a heavy gun leaves a kill that a lighter ready gun can make to that gun and fires elsewhere
1035: function aiPickPrimary(u,ws,focus){ let pv=0,primary=null;
1036:   for(const t of alive('player')){ let sh=0,hull=0; for(const w of ws){ if(!canFire(u,w,t)) continue; const e=expectedVs(u,w,t); sh+=e.shield*.3; hull+=e.hull; } if(!sh&&!hull) continue;
1037:     const val=(Math.min(hull,t.hp)+sh)*(t.isSq?.6:1.1)+(hull>=t.hp?25+t.maxHp*.15:0)+(t===focus?10:0)+(1-t.hp/t.maxHp)*15; if(val>pv){pv=val;primary=t;} }
1038:   return primary; }
1039: // shield strippers first: the order key is the share of a gun's fire that comes off shields (ion strips more than it deals), then raw weight
1040: function aiGunOrder(u,ws,t){ const k=new Map(); for(const w of ws){ let v=-1; if(t&&canFire(u,w,t)){ const e=expectedVs(u,w,t); v=e.raw>0?Math.round(e.shield/e.raw*4):0; } k.set(w,v); }
1041:   return ws.slice().sort((a,b)=>k.get(b)-k.get(a)||(b.dmg*b.shots)-(a.dmg*a.shots)); }
1042: async function aiVolley(u,live){
1043:     const vet=aiVet(), focus=vet&&B.aiFocus&&!B.aiFocus.dead?B.aiFocus:null, tried=new Set();
1044:     let fired=false;
1045:     for(let g=0;g<u.weapons.length;g++){
1046:       if(B.over) return null; if(u.dead) break;
1047:       const ws=u.weapons.filter(w=>weaponReady(w)&&w.k!=='flak'&&!tried.has(w)); if(!ws.length) break;
1048:       const primary=aiPickPrimary(u,ws,focus); // veterans concentrate on it; both levels use it to order the guns
1049:       const w=aiGunOrder(u,ws,primary)[0], wi=u.weapons.indexOf(w); tried.add(w);
1050:       let bt=null, bv=0;
1051:       const taunt=alive('player').find(p=>!p.isSq&&p.fx&&p.fx.taunt&&hdist(p,u)<=p.fx.taunt); /* Aspect of the Bulwark: ships in its reach must fire on it */
1052:       if(taunt&&canFire(u,w,taunt)){ bt=taunt; bv=1; }
1053:       else if(vet&&primary&&canFire(u,w,primary)&&!(w.vsSq>1&&!primary.isSq&&alive('player').some(s=>s.isSq&&canFire(u,w,s)))){ bt=primary; bv=1;
1054:         if(expectedVs(u,w,primary).hull>=primary.hp*2&&ws.some(x=>x!==w&&canFire(u,x,primary)&&expectedVs(u,x,primary).hull>=primary.hp)){ let av=3;
1055:           for(const t of alive('player')){ if(t===primary||!canFire(u,w,t)) continue; const e=expectedVs(u,w,t); const v=Math.min(e.hull,t.hp)+e.shield*.3+(e.hull>=t.hp?20:0); if(v>av){av=v;bt=t;} } } }
1056:       else for(const t of alive('player')){ if(!canFire(u,w,t)) continue; const e=expectedVs(u,w,t); let v=Math.min(e.hull,t.hp)+e.shield*.3; if(e.hull>=t.hp) v+=20; if(!t.isSq) v*=1.1; if(v>bv){bv=v;bt=t;} }
1057:       if(bt && bv>0.5){ await fireWeapon(u,wi,bt,{called:null}); fired=true; await wait(30); if(!live()) return null; }
1058:     }
1059:     return fired;
1060: }
1061: async function aiTurn(){
1062:   const order=alive('enemy').sort((a,b)=>(a.isSq?0:1)-(b.isSq?0:1) || b.speed-a.speed);
1063:   for(const u of order){ if(B.over) return; if(u.dead||!u.isSq) continue; await aiUnit(u); }
1064:   // v53-D: Classic mode picks each next ship by opportunity (aiPickActivation, veteran); standard keeps the fixed speed order
1065:   const done=new Set(); for(let g=0;g<order.length;g++){ if(B.over) return; const left=order.filter(u=>!u.isSq&&!u.dead&&!done.has(u)); if(!left.length) break; const u=aiPickActivation(left); done.add(u); await aiUnit(u); }
1066: }
1067: // true travel distance (in hexes, around holes, rocks and wrecks) from every hex to the nearest of the given targets

1069:   for(let i=0;i<q.length;i++){ const h=q[i], d=D.get(hkey(h.q,h.r)); for(const dd of DIRS){ const n={q:h.q+dd[0],r:h.r+dd[1]}, k=hkey(n.q,n.r); if(D.has(k)||!onBoard(n.q,n.r)||blocks(terr(n.q,n.r))) continue; D.set(k,d+1); q.push(n); } }
1070:   return D; }
1071: function fieldDist(D,v,fallback){ const d=D.get(hkey(v.q,v.r)); return d==null?fallback+6:d; }
1072: // v53-D: the ability checks in one helper. 'pre' runs before moving; 'aim' re-runs the ones that depend on where the ship ended up (focus and frenzy

1091: async function aiUnit(u){

1093:   if(u.side==='enemy'&&!B.cmdEUsed&&B.cmdENext!=null){ await bossCmdFire(); if(B.over||u.dead) return; }

1101:     if(!u.isSq&&!runner) await tacticRam(u); if(B.over||u.dead||!live()) return;

1109:     for(const s of states){

2: // v41: seeded rules randomness (mulberry32). Each battle reseeds from B.seed, so a seed plus the same moves replays a fight;
3: // the simulator takes SEED from the environment so whole runs repeat exactly. Cosmetic effects keep Math.random.
4: let RNG_S=(Math.random()*4294967296)>>>0;
5: function rndSeed(s){ RNG_S=(s>>>0); }
6: function rnd(){ RNG_S=(RNG_S+0x6D2B79F5)|0; let t=Math.imul(RNG_S^(RNG_S>>>15),1|RNG_S); t=(t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),61|t))^t; return ((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296; }
7: const ri = (a,b) => a + Math.floor(rnd()*(b-a+1));
8: const pick = a => a[Math.floor(rnd()*a.length)];
9: const shuffle = a => { for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(rnd()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]];} return a; };

35: function cubeRound(x,z){let y=-x-z, rx=Math.round(x), rz=Math.round(z), ry=Math.round(y);
36:   const dx=Math.abs(rx-x), dy=Math.abs(ry-y), dz=Math.abs(rz-z);

9: function botObj(v){ const o=B.obj; if(!o) return 0; const col=h=>h.q+Math.floor(h.r/2);
10:   if(o.type==='hold') return hdist(v,o.hex)*1.6;

13:   if(o.type==='intercept'){ const tr=alive('enemy').filter(x=>x.transport); return tr.length?Math.min(...tr.map(t=>hdist(v,t)))*2.5:0; }
14:   if(o.type==='hunt'){ const f=alive('enemy').find(x=>x.flagship); return f?hdist(v,f)*2.5:0; }

16: async function botTurn(){ await swarmPhase('player');
17:   for(const u of alive('player').filter(x=>!x.isSq)){
18:     if(B.over||u.dead) continue;
19:     if(u.ab && canAbility(u)){ if(u.ab==='launch') await useAbility(u,{hex:launchHexes(u)[0],kind:rnd()<.5?'fighter':'bomber'});
20:       else if(u.ab==='nova'){ const t=novaTargets(u).find(h=>alive('enemy').some(e=>hdist(e,h)<=1)&&!alive('player').some(p=>hdist(p,h)<=1)); if(t) await useAbility(u,{hex:t}); }
21:       else await useAbility(u); }
22:     const foes=alive('enemy'); if(!foes.length) break;
23:     const {states}=computeReach(u); let best=null,bs=-1e9;
24:     const PFb=pathField(foes.filter(f=>!f.isSq)); for(const s of states){ const v=stateView(u,s); const off=evalOffense(v,u.weapons.filter(weaponReady),foes); const th=evalThreat(v,foes); const hd=Math.min(...foes.map(f=>hdist(v,f))), nd=Math.max(hd,fieldDist(PFb,v,hd)); const sc=off-th*.3-(off>0?0:nd*3)-botObj(v); if(sc>bs){bs=sc;best=s;} }
25:     if(best&&best.path.length) await moveUnit(u,best);
26:     for(let i=0;i<u.weapons.length;i++){ let bt=null,bv=0; for(const t of alive('enemy')){ if(canFire(u,u.weapons[i],t)){ const e=expectedVs(u,u.weapons[i],t); const v=e.hull+e.shield*.3+(e.hull>=t.hp?20:0); if(v>bv){bv=v;bt=t;} } } if(bt) await fireWeapon(u,i,bt); if(B.over) return; }
27:   }

573:   if(global.SQD) for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(global.SQD)) console.log('   ',k.padEnd(22),'dealt/battle',(v.dealt/n).toFixed(0),'units/battle',(v.n/n).toFixed(1),'died',(v.died/v.n*100).toFixed(0)+'%');
574:   console.log(`${S.tag} s${sector}: win ${wins}/${n} (${Math.round(wins/n*100)}%), hull left ${(hp/n*100).toFixed(0)}%, lost ${(lost/n).toFixed(1)}, rounds ${(rounds/n).toFixed(1)}`);
575: })();

===== ANNEX: simulation summary (document, cite by name) =====
# Sim matrix at scale, plus harness defects

Run Oct 23, 2026, on repo main at eca489e, at the owner's request while his other model quotas reset. Method: the game's own harness (sim3d.js under Node 24), every mode run once at the sizes below, output kept verbatim in the run log. Balance numbers are bot versus bot with the harness's standard bot on both sides unless a mode says otherwise; treat them as directional, not as human play.

## Correctness: the net is clean

- fuzz 300 battles: exceptions 0, invariant breaks 0, average 6.6 rounds.
- booncheck: all pass (every boon and aspect spot-checked by the harness produces its stated effect, including duo boon offers at 25 of 60 once both patrons are held).
- cmdcheck: all pass (all 24 admirals' Commands fire, respect the once-per-battle rule, and keep weapon state).
- progcheck: PASSED. rulecheck: PASSED.
- capcheck: displayed capture odds match rolled odds (shown 82% vs rolled 82.3%; shown 59% vs rolled 59.4%; the 100% cases roll 100%).
- stalecheck: no stale-unit actions across battle boundaries; battles differ as expected.
- mapcheck, 300 sectors: every pre-boss column offers a fight (0 missing), and all 900 pre-boss nodes are rest nodes (drydock 306, field 297, shipyard 297).
- enccount, 300 sectors: encounter offers are near-uniform across the eight types (rout 331, flagship 315, ambush 299, hold 298, defend 296, intercept 288, break 275, hunt 264).
- budgetcheck: enemy budgets scale from about 4.0 CP at sector 1 column 0 to about 19.8 at sector 3 column 6 under the new formula, consistently above the old flat numbers in every cell.

## Faction and fleet matrices

fmatrix 12 (FLEET_LISTS fleets, 432 battles, 3 timeouts): row win rates against the field are compressed (most cells 58 to 83), with two outliers worth a look: Hegemony fleet lists beat Voidsworn lists in 100% of reps, and Chorus lists beat Voidsworn only 17% of the time. Per-hull damage dealt: the Deathless harvester is the top hull by a wide margin (31,819 damage over 152 deployments), then the Chorus brood (21,861 over 84) and the Hegemony vanguard (17,419 over 85). Per-weapon total damage: gauss first (50,154 over 4,451 shots), turret second by volume (32,243 over 2,937 shots), squadron bombs third (26,722). Boarding is nearly absent in this mode (player 6, enemy 0 across 432 battles).

matrix 10 sk (admiral starting fleets, 360 battles, 0 timeouts): average win rates by attacking faction: Corsair 98, Ithar 90, Chorus 88, Voidsworn 80, Hegemony 55, Deathless 50. Per battle: 6.9 rounds, 1.9 rams, 0.5 boardings, 0.20 captures, 17.0 sections lost. Note the inversion against fmatrix for Hegemony (strongest fleet lists there, second weakest starting fleets here): the two modes draw fleets from different sources, so neither table should be used to tune the other.

bossmatrix 8 (sector 1 boss, each admiral's fleet converted to their own faction): admiral win rates spread from 38% (Grax) and 40% (Sekhmar) to 98% (Maren) and 98% (Voice), with Vesh and Mending at 90%. By boss faction, the player win rate is: Hegemony boss 51%, Chorus 63%, Deathless 71%, Ithar 74%, Corsair 76%, Voidsworn 80%. Carrier fleets average 74%, gun fleets 67%. Read together: at sector 1 the admiral pick alone is worth roughly 30 points of boss win rate, which makes the admiral screen a hidden difficulty setting, and the Hegemony boss is the sector 1 wall.

## Campaign shape (campaign 30, harness patched, see defects)

Thirty runs per cell with the harness's fixed per-sector fleets:

| Cell | Win | Rounds | Hull left | Ships lost | Anim wait (new clock) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 battle (col 1) | 100% | 4.1 | 75% | 0.6 | 40s |
| S1 battle (col 4) | 100% | 4.9 | 66% | 1.0 | 49s |
| S1 elite | 73% | 6.4 | 38% | 2.1 | 60s |
| S1 boss | 77% | 10.0 | 35% | 2.2 | 73s |
| S2 battle (col 1) | 93% | 6.9 | 54% | 2.4 | 79s |
| S2 battle (col 4) | 83% | 7.1 | 47% | 2.4 | 83s |
| S2 elite | 57% | 8.1 | 23% | 3.8 | 95s |
| S2 boss | 87% | 9.9 | 40% | 3.0 | 93s |
| S3 battle (col 1) | 93% | 5.8 | 65% | 1.9 | 94s |
| S3 battle (col 4) | 83% | 7.2 | 51% | 2.6 | 114s |
| S3 elite | 53% | 7.5 | 40% | 3.2 | 120s |
| S3 boss | 100% | 9.6 | 64% | 1.9 | 109s |

Zero timeouts in all 360 battles. The shape confirms the council gamer lens at three times its sample: sector 1 battles are a formality, the elite cells are the wall (S2 elite is the single hardest cell in the game for these fleets), and the S3 boss 100% is an artifact of the scripted dreadnought fleet rather than a soft boss. Animation wait is the other confirmed problem: even on the new clock, elite and boss fights in sectors 2 and 3 carry 95 to 120 seconds of pure animation time (the old clock prices the same fights at 160 to 213 seconds).

## Archetypes (archmatrix, reduced)

archMatrix at its default size did not finish (see defects). A reduced run (n=1, 480 battles aggregated, sector 2, column 3) gives player win rates by enemy archetype: none 90% (6.3 rounds, 4.2 enemy ships), heavy 89% (6.5 rounds, 3.3 ships), carrier 87% (6.7 rounds, 3.5 ships), swarm 85% (5.8 rounds, 5.9 ships). No archetype is an outlier; swarm fields the most ships and still loses most often, which is a mild surprise worth a human look before any tuning.

## Harness defects found by this run (tooling, not game code)

1. campaignSim crashes intermittently. It pre-registers objective bookkeeping only for the objectives it picks itself (rout, flagship, hold, and rout for bosses), but the campaign battle it launches can roll any of the eight encounter types. When the rolled type is ambush, defend, intercept, break or hunt, the post-battle accounting reads an undefined entry and throws a TypeError, killing the whole run. OBSERVED at sim3d.js campaignSim, the line that increments OBJS_SEEN for B.obj.type after a win. The one-line guard used for the rerun (in a scratch copy only, the repo file was not edited): register the entry before incrementing, i.e. `(OBJS_SEEN[B.obj.type]=OBJS_SEEN[B.obj.type]||{n:0,w:0}).w++`. Until that lands, campaign results will keep dying at random depending on the encounter roll, which is a plausible explanation for flaky campaign numbers in past sessions.
2. archMatrix is impractical at its default workload: 4 archetypes x 24 admirals x 5 enemy factions x n battles is 1,920 battles at n=4, and it produced no output inside a 20-minute limit. Either the default n needs to come down or the mode needs progress output so a long run is distinguishable from a hung one.

## Not checked

Human play (all numbers are the harness bot on both sides, whose player-side decisions are documented in the engineer lens report to differ from the veteran AI the enemy uses). The campaign table uses the harness's fixed fleets, not admiral starting fleets or grown fleets. fmatrix and matrix disagree by construction (different fleet sources) and were not reconciled here. No mode was run at Heat above 0.
